# KiroBucket Manifest View

## Newest first

Manifest Added is the date the manifest first entered this bucket's Git history; later edits do not change it, and renames are followed where Git can detect them. Program Update is the latest published upstream release date, falling back to the downloadable asset's Last-Modified date. A dash means a date was unavailable.
Download Size is measured from direct download URLs' HTTP Content-Length headers. Architecture-specific packages are listed separately; a dash means the source did not provide a size.
Installer Type is inferred from manifest metadata and the declared download format; unknown EXE installer engines are labeled unverified, not guessed.
